**关键原始错误**

```text
2026-11-13T04:12:54.6925228Z error: Failed to build `vllm-ascend @ file:///vllm-workspace/vllm-ascend`
2026-11-13T04:12:54.7096659Z              error: downloading 'https://gitcode.com/cann-src-third-party/makeself/releases/download/release-2.5.0-patch1.0/makeself-release-2.5.0-patch1.tar.gz' failed
2026-11-13T04:12:54.7097506Z                    status_code: 22
2026-11-13T04:12:54.7134712Z            HTTP/1.1 503 Service Unavailable
2026-11-13T04:12:54.7136722Z            X-Squid-Error: ERR_CONNECT_FAIL 110
2026-11-13T05:01:25.0216761Z E               RuntimeError: Timeout: these nodes did not become ready: ['http://0.0.0.0:36259/health'] in time: 2800s
```

**原因分析**

main安装vllm-ascend时下载gitcode makeself归档返回HTTP503及Squid ERR_CONNECT_FAIL110，直接阻断editable构建。已证实该main运行中的依赖下载/代理连接失败，未断定代理或上游哪端最终责任；随后health2800秒超时因果未单独证明，不将其当独立根因。

**证据与基线**

- 失败SHA：671292ee7ee2c6a620f307ce7930d84d772f4d5a；workflow：.github/workflows/schedule_nightly_test_a3_560t.yaml；event：workflow_dispatch；branch：main。

**关键原始错误**

```text
2026-11-13T05:57:29.0779988Z Failed to obtain the console log level. The possible causes are as follows:
2026-11-13T05:57:29.0780836Z 1. Different containers share the same device;
2026-11-13T05:57:29.0781580Z 2. Failed to open the character device when interacting with the OS kernel;
2026-11-13T05:57:29.0782380Z 3. Failed to obtain the number of devices.
2026-11-13T05:57:29.0783094Z dcmi model initialized failed, because the device is used. ret is -8020
  Failed to obtain the console log level. The possible causes are as follows:
  1. Different containers share the same device;
  2. Failed to open the character device when interacting with the OS kernel;
  3. Failed to obtain the number of devices.
  dcmi model initialized failed, because the device is used. ret is -8020
```

**原因分析**

实际main运行的两个a2b1b3单卡runner在npu-smi预检阶段DCMI初始化-8020，设备访问不可用发生在测试代码执行前。独立main证据确认共享设备环境故障；具体设备占用、字符设备映射、驱动或设备数量原因仍未证实。不混入历史PR-only FED A3模式，也不将此前未比较PR环境的unknown条目追溯认定为infra。

- 失败SHA：671292ee7ee2c6a620f307ce7930d84d772f4d5a；workflow：.github/workflows/schedule_daily_test.yaml；event：workflow_dispatch；branch：main。

```

**原因分析**

main的A5 Qwen3-32B测试启动过程中HcclAllGather报告EI0006 socket获取超时，随后health端点在2800秒内未就绪。main运行身份明确，但日志列出远端算子未发起/启动过晚、NPU异常或链路配置等多种原因，无法区分代码时序与设备/网络根因，因此不凭timeout断定infra。更早cache restore MODULE_NOT_FOUND被容错继续，并非最终pytest失败的已证实原因。

<!-- job-time finding=F-014 job=131690719328 at=2026-11-13T04:43:31.3314057Z -->

**关键原始错误**

```text
2026-11-13T04:43:31.3314057Z tests/ut/worker/test_attn_utils_v2.py::test_v2_fused_offload_prepares_request_ownership_and_restores_tails FAILED
2026-11-13T04:43:54.2574618Z E           AttributeError: 'AscendModelState' object has no attribute '_offload_pool_generations'
2026-11-13T04:43:54.3087948Z E       AttributeError: 'AscendModelState' object has no attribute '_offload_stable_prefixes'
2026-11-13T04:43:56.5970097Z E       AssertionError: assert {'copy_sfa_dr...refixes', ...} == {'copy_sfa_dr...token_to_req'}
2026-11-13T04:44:01.2996105Z FAILED tests/ut/worker/v2/test_sparse_kv_offload_metadata.py::test_full_graph_metadata_preserves_live_ids_pool_cpu_views_and_step - AssertionError: assert {'copy_sfa_dr...refixes', ...} == {'copy_sfa_dr...token_to_req'}
  FAILED tests/ut/worker/v2/test_sparse_kv_offload_metadata.py::test_full_graph_metadata_preserves_live_ids_pool_cpu_views_and_step - AssertionError: assert {'copy_sfa_dr...refixes', ...} == {'copy_sfa_dr...token_to_req'}
```

**原因分析**

CPU单测逐Job确认AscendModelState缺少_offload_pool_generations/_offload_stable_prefixes属性及metadata集合断言失败；较早两个Job还报告PrivateUse1HooksInterface未注册。复用已有offload单测适配失败模式，保留各Job实际差异，不宣称所有异常共享深层根因；未核对精确PR/main责任。

### F-036

<!-- finding F-036 key=e2e7b35ef42d55dbfb11d4de732e663d896db8d53cc96c9623bfd363c2e7fd58 discovered=2026-11-13T04:01:46.026263Z source=2026-11-13 -->

**关键原始错误**

```text
2026-11-13T03:20:59.4047278Z tests/ut/worker/test_mtp_pcp_speculator_v2.py::test_non_pcp_draft_prefill_normalizes_main_target_graph_padding[query_ends0-NONE] FAILED
2026-11-13T03:21:07.5036156Z E       assert [2, 2, 2, 2] == [2, 4, 6, 8]
2026-11-13T03:21:13.6125050Z FAILED tests/ut/worker/test_mtp_pcp_speculator_v2.py::test_non_pcp_draft_prefill_normalizes_main_target_graph_padding[query_ends0-NONE] - assert [2, 2, 2, 2] == [2, 4, 6, 8]
2026-11-13T03:21:13.6134345Z FAILED tests/ut/worker/test_mtp_pcp_speculator_v2.py::test_non_pcp_draft_prefill_normalizes_main_target_graph_padding[query_ends0-PIECEWISE] - assert [2, 2, 2, 2] == [2, 4, 6, 8]

<!-- finding F-004 key=6874719bb08fa30f1b8cd34541e81819c895280a3f39b17803bdf816faed7d8a discovered=2026-11-13T04:01:16.479938Z source=2026-11-13 -->

**关键原始错误**

```text
2026-11-13T03:38:16.6093789Z E       AttributeError: 'DeepseekV41Model' object has no attribute 'skip_padding_tokens_in_moe'
2026-11-13T03:38:22.2787151Z FAILED tests/ut/models/test_engram_multistream.py::test_sp_row_copy_waits_for_its_table - AttributeError: 'DeepseekV41Model' object has no attribute 'skip_padding_tokens_in_moe'

**原因分析**

CPU单测test_sp_row_copy_waits_for_its_table访问DeepseekV41Model缺失的skip_padding_tokens_in_moe属性而失败。已逐Job核实相同异常；测试中的模拟upload/参数错误不是该Job的失败原因。尚未完成精确PR/main差异核对，不推测具体责��PR。

### F-026

<!-- finding F-026 key=b2274b986aef2a5252165bb744a3eae5de93cbc780a8b6204daccc6974dfdf69 discovered=2026-11-13T07:01:24.808910Z source=2026-11-13 -->
<!-- first-error finding=F-026 at=2026-11-13T04:37:52.9126252Z -->
<!-- job-time finding=F-026 job=131684542967 at=2026-11-13T04:37:52.9126252Z -->

**关键原始错误**

```text
2026-11-13T04:37:52.9126252Z (EngineCore pid=18754) ERROR 11-13 04:37:52 [core.py:1632] RuntimeError: CPU weight offload is incompatible with ACL graph capture for 28 offloaded parameter(s) stored in FRACTAL_NZ: the prefetch copy (ND pinned CPU storage -> NZ static buffer) requires an ND->NZ conversion, which is aclop-only on the current CANN/torch_npu versions; running aclop operators during NPU graph capture is not supported yet by these libraries. Use enforce_eager=True, or disable NZ weights (additional_config={'weight_nz_mode': 0}).
2026-11-13T04:37:52.9173644Z RuntimeError: CPU weight offload is incompatible with ACL graph capture for 28 offloaded parameter(s) stored in FRACTAL_NZ: the prefetch copy (ND pinned CPU storage -> NZ static buffer) requires an ND->NZ conversion, which is aclop-only on the current CANN/torch_npu versions; running aclop operators during NPU graph capture is not supported yet by these libraries. Use enforce_eager=True, or disable NZ weights (additional_config={'weight_nz_mode': 0}).
2026-11-13T04:38:06.7313653Z E               RuntimeError: Engine core initialization failed. See root cause above. Failed core proc(s): {}
```

**原因分析**

设备单测test_prefetch_offload_nz_graph_accuracy启动时明确拒绝CPU offload与FRACTAL_NZ ACL graph组合：28个参数的ND到NZ预取需要aclop而当前CANN/torch_npu不支持捕获。日志建议enforce_eager=True或weight_nz_mode=0。配置/能力不兼容已证明，未核对PR/main差异，非已证明硬件故障。

**证据与基线**

dorny/paths-filter@v4.0.2拒绝predicate-quantifier=some-with-excludes，实际支持值仅every/some。动作输入与版本能力不兼容，精确PR/main归属未核对。

**原因分析**

mypy报告attn_utils.py将CacheOwner赋给str变量，随后访问slot/role属性失败。明确静态类型契约问题，精确main/PR归属未核实。

```text
2026-11-13T10:34:42.6445870Z (APIServer pid=32288) ERROR 11-13 10:34:42 [async_llm.py:943] AttributeError: 'tokenizers.Tokenizer' object has no attribute '_fast'
2026-11-13T10:34:42.6510409Z (APIServer pid=32288) ERROR 11-13 10:34:42 [vllm_error.py:54] AttributeError: 'tokenizers.Tokenizer' object has no attribute '_fast'
2026-11-13T10:34:42.6544951Z [2026-11-13 10:34:42] [ERROR] dispatch_tests failed: Error code: 500

Exit task because all warmup requests failed, failed reasons: {"After 2 retries, request failed with exception: ClientConnectorError: Cannot connect to host localhost:33899 ssl:default [Connect call failed ('127.0.0.1', 33899)]": 1} | Visit https://ais-bench-benchmark.readthedocs.io/zh-cn/latest/faqs/error_codes.html#tinfer-runtime-001 for further help.
